w:rsidR="00B8302A" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00790AD0" w:rsidP="002F16D3"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading1"/></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="5" w:name="_Toc230880916"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:lastRenderedPageBreak/><w:t xml:space="preserve">Solution </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="000562F1" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>o</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>verview</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="5"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="5E1F9C4D" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="007A0B18" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="007A0B18" w:rsidP="007A0B18"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The AR Intelligence solution is designed to transform Accounts Receivable from a reactive reporting function into a proactive, data-driven strategy. It addresses the critical challenge of lacking predictive visibility into cash flow, which directly impacts liquidity management, forecasting accuracy, and working capital efficiency. By replacing manual, judgment-based processes with AI-empowered tools, the primary objective is to optimize cash flow, sharpen forecast accuracy, and prioritize collection efforts for maximum impact.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="1DDAC056" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="007A0B18" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="007A0B18" w:rsidP="007A0B18"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The solution provides unified AR Intelligence on a cohesive SAP Business Data Cloud (BDC) landscape, moving far beyond static reporting to offer real-time, predictive insights. The architecture seamlessly orchestrates several powerful SAP components:Â SAP DatasphereÂ is used for data integration and creating a governed semantic layer for consistent, reusable analytics;Â SAP DatabricksÂ is leveraged for developing and hosting sophisticated machine learning models for payment prediction and risk scoring; andÂ SAP Analytics Cloud (SAC)Â serves as the interactive front-end for dynamic dashboards, reporting, and self-service analytics.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="7E645BEF" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="000F1EAE" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="000F1EAE" w:rsidP="000F1EAE"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Predictive Intelligence is a core capability, utilizing machine learning to forecast when open invoices will be paid based on historical payment behaviors. This enables the system to automatically generate aÂ Prioritized Collections Worklist, ensuring the team focuses on high-value, high-risk accounts. Furthermore, the solution offersÂ Interactive Analysis, allowing users to drill down from high-level KPIs to specific customer and invoice details to find the root causes of delays. It also featuresÂ AI-Powered Business InsightsÂ through natural language queries ('Just Ask') for instant visual answers and 'Smart Insights' that automatically analyze data to explain metric changes.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="450F86A9" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="000F1EAE" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="000F1EAE" w:rsidP="00B8302A"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>By empowering finance teams to anticipate cash flow and identify risks before they escalate, AR Intelligence delivers significant business impact. It enables proactive collections, optimizes cash inflows, and reduces overdue exposure, leading to improved cash realization. This strategic approach moves an organization from reactive problem-solving to proactive, data-driven decision-making, ensuring greater financial stability and control.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w:rsidRDefault="00B616E0" w:rsidP="00B616E0"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0"/><w:numId w:val="13"/></w:numPr><w:ind w:right="4115"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Machine Learning Payment Forecasting: </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The system continuously analyzes historical payment data to generate predictive models, forecasting the exact likelihood of on-time payments for every open invoice.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="6418A585" w14:textId="77777777" w14:textId="3F7A481C" w:rsidR="00323C03" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00611131" w:rsidP="002F16D3"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading1"/></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="7" w:id="7"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="5ED9EC4F" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="005B520C" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="005B520C" w:rsidP="005B520C"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>TheÂ KPMG Target Operating Model (TOM)Â is a holistic framework that defines how an organization operates and delivers value. It shapes business transformation across every layer of the enterprise, providing a proven, future-ready solution set built on KPMGâ€™s functional and industry-leading practices. Comprising six distinct design layers, the TOM ensures that strategic changes are seamlessly codified and implemented across the entire organization.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="2F616740" w14:textId="23E5C5F5" w:rsidR="00082926" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00082926" w:rsidP="00ED4CA6"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:hyperlink r:id="rId28" w:tgtFrame="_blank" w:tooltip="https://spo-global.kpmg.com/sites/GO-OI-SVC-Advisory-Velocity" w:history="1"><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:rStyle w:val="Hyperlink"/></w:rPr><w:t>Velocity</w:t></w:r></w:hyperlink><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">Â works </w:t></w:r><w:proofErr w:type="gramStart"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>hand-in-hand</w:t></w:r><w:proofErr w:type="gramEnd"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> with the Target Operating Model (TOM) to translate strategic design into execution. By combining global TOM designs and tailored value stream blueprints</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="001C57C3"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> - </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>spanning various industries, sectors, and technologies</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="001C57C3"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> - </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>with Velocityâ€™s pre-configured journeys, enablers, and automation, organizations can significantly accelerate their transformation. This integration ensures that strategic design and technical delivery remain seamlessly connected, presenting users with the exact TOM enablers required for their specific platform, sector, or functional journey.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="675176FB" w14:textId="4AE85FA9" w:rsidR="00323C03" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00C91228" w:rsidP="00F93488"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/></w:rPr><w:t>Use case impacts on the design layer of the TOM</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="8"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="65D1B1FF" w14:textId="60A0D998" w:rsidR="00ED4CA6" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> </w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="775ADB28" w14:textId="40CEA9B7" w:rsidR="00C77A99" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00C2644E" w:rsidP="00C77A99"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/><w:spacing w:after="240"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="9" w:name="_Toc230880920"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/></w:rPr><w:t>TOM layer details: AR Intelligence</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="9"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="78167BAE" w14:textId="61180DF5" w:rsidR="00CB5E48" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00CB5E48" w:rsidP="00821C27"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The Account Receivable (AR) Intelligence solution through the framework of a Target Operating Model (TOM). It outlines six distinct operating model layers</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="001C57C3"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> - </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Functional Processes, People, Service Delivery, Technology, Performance Insights, and Governance</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="001C57C3"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> - </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>and describes the specific assets and capabilities that the AR Intelligence solution delivers within each layer. The overarching theme is a fundamental shift in the functional process, moving from reactive aging analysis to a proactive, risk-based collections strategy. This transformation is driven by three core pillars: unifying AR data into a single analytical layer, applying machine learning models for predictive analysis of payment likelihood and risk, and generating dynamic worklists to direct collection teams to the highest-risk accounts.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="62607D72" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="002C4405" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="002C4405" w:rsidP="002C4405"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">The solution is designed to empower different user groups. For theÂ PeopleÂ layer, it provides CFOs and Controllers with real-time visibility for strategic decisions on working capital, while Collections Managers can use ML-driven worklists to guide team efforts and improve efficiency. TheÂ Service Delivery ModelÂ is a centralized, self-service analytics platform delivered through an interactive SAP Analytics Cloud dashboard. This gives business users direct, on-demand access to trusted data and insights, significantly </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:lastRenderedPageBreak/><w:t>reducing their dependency on IT for standard reporting and ad-hoc queries. This model ensures that actionable intelligence is readily available to those who need it.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="698F06D5" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="002C4405" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="002C4405" w:rsidP="00821C27"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>TheÂ TechnologyÂ layer is built on a unified cloud architecture using the SAP Business Data Cloud. This ecosystem leveragesÂ SAP DatasphereÂ for data integration and a governed semantic layer,Â SAP DatabricksÂ for developing and hosting the machine learning models, andÂ SAP Analytics CloudÂ as the interactive front-end for dashboards and analytics. This robust architecture provides a single source of truth for all AR data, delivering real-time KPIs, predictive insights, and operational data with drill-down capabilities. Finally, theÂ GovernanceÂ layer is embedded via the SAP Datasphere semantic layer, which guarantees consistent data definitions, standardized KPIs, and trusted, reusable analytics across the enterprise.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="4328B985" w14:textId="662DC3C6" w:rsidR="00C77A99" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00B36B9A" w:rsidP="00C77A99"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/><w:spacing w:after="240"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="10" w:name="_Toc230880921"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/></w:rPr><w:t>KPMG leading practices and design decisions â€“ Details</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="10"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="14FDF4EB" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00946F73" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00946F73" w:rsidP="00B36B9A"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The leading practices are centered on transforming AR from a reactive, manual process into a proactive, data-driven function. On the process side, this involves shifting from aging reports to forward-looking strategies (Proactive AR), using dynamic, multi-factor risk scores to drive collections (Smart Prioritization), and empowering business users with analytics to reduce IT reliance (Self-Service). Technologically, these practices are supported by unifying AR data into aÂ Single Source of TruthÂ to eliminate manual reconciliation, leveragingÂ Predictive Machine LearningÂ to forecast payment behaviors, and embedding role-based dashboards directly into user workflows (Embedded Analytics). The potential benefits of adopting these practices include lower DSO and better cash flow, higher collection efficiency, and enhanced visibility for strategic working capital decisions.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="17AD7742" w14:textId="0FC06966" w:rsidR="00F6525B" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00F6525B" w:rsidP="00F6525B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">The specific design considerations detail how these leading practices are implemented. The process is built aroundÂ ML-Prioritized Workflows, where collection activities are designed around a dynamic worklist prioritized by ML-driven risk scores from </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="001C51D9" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">SAP </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Databricks, fundamentally shifting the focus from static aging to predictive risk. It also incorporates natural language queries like "Just Ask" and "Smart Insights" throughÂ JouleÂ on SAC stories. Technologically, the architecture is built on a suite of SAP products: pulling AR data directly fromÂ S/4HANAÂ Data Products, usingÂ SAP DatasphereÂ to build a centralized and governed semantic layer, leveragingÂ SAP DatabricksÂ to develop ML models with secure, zero-copy Delta Sharing, and usingÂ SAP Analytics CloudÂ as a live-connected, interactive front-end.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="73B1C47A" w14:textId="49C952FE" w:rsidR="00B36B9A" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00F6525B" w:rsidP="00F6525B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Ultimately, these design choices result in significant benefits. They create aÂ Scalable ArchitectureÂ with a clean separation between data warehousing and advanced analytics, ensuring the solution can grow with the business. They enforceÂ Strong GovernanceÂ by creating a single version of the truth through the semantic layer, which guarantees data consistency and reliability. Finally, the solution deliversÂ Real-Time Insights, providing immediate, decision-ready data through live connections, which empowers the organization to act with speed and confidence.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="2CE96085" w14:textId="4B81C4D2" w:rsidR="00D451B0" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="006476BA" w:rsidP="00D451B0"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">This </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="0068382B" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>demonstration is structured as a â€œDay-in-the-Lifeâ€ journey. It begins with the CFO identifying a global DSO leak on the executive dashboard, transitions to the Collections Manager deploying an automated, prioritized worklist to mitigate the issue, and concludes with an analyst using natural language queries to pinpoint root-cause payment barriers.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="69D9433C" w14:textId="2B230693" w:rsidR="00323C03" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00F311C8" w:rsidP="00FF2931"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="14" w:id="16"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="17"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="18"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="19"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="20"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="21"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="22"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="23"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="24"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="25"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="26"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="27"/><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="28"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The AR Intelligence demo illustrates the solution's capacity to transition finance organizations from reactive tracking to proactive, data-driven decision-making. The script is tailored for two primary personas: theÂ CFO/Financial ControllerÂ (high-level oversight) and theÂ Financial ManagerÂ (granular analysis).</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="47F76F08" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00175221" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00175221" w:rsidP="00175221"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>To ensure a seamless presentation, several technical prerequisites must be met: the SAP Datasphere model must be refreshed, the SAP Databricks ML models must be run to generate current predictions, and the SAP Analytics Cloud (SAC) environment must be opened to the main dashboard.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="37C4EFD8" w14:textId="2419BC6F" w:rsidR="00323C03" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00F311C8" w:rsidP="00F14E2B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="29" w:name="_Toc230880926"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>Approach</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="29"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="377AB269" w14:textId="2D01A848" w:rsidR="00175221" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00175221" w:rsidP="00F14E2B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Batang"/><w:lang w:eastAsia="ko-KR"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The demo employs a â€œtop-downâ€ narrative, starting with an executive-level view and drilling down into operational details. A central theme is emphasizing the system as a â€œSingle Source of Truthâ€ by showing both personas using the same unified dashboard to ensure data consistency. The presentation is focused on three core value pillars: real-time visualization, predictive risk modeling, and self-service analytics.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="79F0639D" w14:textId="3A9857A3" w:rsidR="00F311C8" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00F311C8" w:rsidP="00F14E2B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New steps</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="30"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="2E027112" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00685AC8" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00685AC8" w:rsidP="00685AC8"><w:pPr><w:pStyle critical AR metrics (DSO, On-Time Payment Rate, Total Overdue) on the main dashboard, including geographic performance analysis using the interactive map</w:t></w:r><w:r </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="00100EEB" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The presenter analyzes the drivers behind late payments, highlighting SACâ€™s ability to automatically identify and visualize the top contributing customers or document types without manual data manipulation</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="217A4349" w14:textId="676AB6C9" highlights user empowerment with the â€œJust Askâ€ natural language query tool, showing how users can input conversational questions to instantly generate visual insights and explore data</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="38F66594" w14:textId="21F9F6D0" w:rsidR="00821C27" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="003446BC" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0"/><w:numId w:val="6"/></w:numPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Phase 4: </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="0069002F" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>Proactive Collections Action</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>.</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="0069002F" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The focus shifts to operational execution by reviewing the prioritized collections worklist. This demonstrates how ML-driven risk scores from SAP Databricks dynamically guide teams to target high-impact accounts</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="79BEDDE7" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00016651" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00016651" w:rsidP="00016651"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading1"/><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="31" w:name="_Toc230880928"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:lastRenderedPageBreak/><w:t>Deployment approach</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="31"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="0EE87488" w14:textId="42553C3D" w:rsidR="0031508D" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="0031508D" w:rsidP="00664C48"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">The implementation of the Account Receivable Intelligence solution follows a rapid, structured </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="00785050" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>hack-to-build</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> deployment methodology. This framework is designed to ensure strict alignment between business objectives and technical execution while minimizing time-to-value. The delivery model is organized into three sequential phases: Discover &amp; Design, Build &amp; Test, and Deploy &amp; Support. Each phase features defined activities and strict milestone gates to ensure quality and project governance.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="3E13FE63" w14:textId="4B58E2BF" w:rsidR="00664C48" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00785050" w:rsidP="00664C48"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>Phase 1: Discover &amp; Design</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="5CE33153" w14:textId="31242584" w:rsidR="00785050" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00785050" w:rsidP="00785050"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The initial phase is dedicated to establishing project alignment, gathering business requirements, and defining the target architecture.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="12E82994" w14:textId="39FBF9EA" w:rsidR="00785050" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00785050" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0"/><w:numId w:val="7"/></w:numPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">Key Activities:Â The project commences with a formal use case </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="000670AB" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Kick-Off</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>, followed by detailed requirements gathering and solution design. During this period, the technical architecture is established, and specific KPIs and dashboard visualizations are defined in collaboration with key stakeholders.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="025556B3" w14:textId="0DBA2E53" w:rsidR="00991A0E" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00785050" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0"/><w:numId w:val="7"/></w:numPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">Milestones &amp; Deliverables:Â Progression to the next phase is contingent upon the completion of key deliverables, including the project </w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="000670AB" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Kick-Off</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve"> Deck, a formalized Technical Architecture document, and officially Signed-off Functional Specifications that serve as the blueprint for development.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="1D3830F0" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00991A0E" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>Phase 2: Build &amp; Test</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="661FB9A5" w14:textId="3DDE88F0" w:rsidR="00362DA4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The core technical development and system validation occur during this intensive phase.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="6A21EA32" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00362DA4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0"/><w:numId w:val="8"/></w:numPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Key Activities:Â Technical teams execute system configuration across SAP Business Technology Platform (BTP) and SAP Databricks. This is followed by complex data integration and modeling, the development of the machine learning predictive models, and the iterative build of the SAP Analytics Cloud (SAC) dashboards. The phase concludes with rigorous User Acceptance Testing (UAT) to validate system accuracy and usability.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="64FDDC44" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00991A0E" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0"/><w:numId w:val="8"/></w:numPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Milestones &amp; Deliverables:Â Critical milestones validating the success of this phase include the readiness of the BTP environment, successful data extraction to the Business Data Cloud (BDC), the delivery of the fully built dashboard, and a completed Test Plan accompanied by formal UAT sign-off.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="4566FAD7" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00991A0E" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>Phase 3: Deploy &amp; Support</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="6F7B5523" w14:textId="1AB1D290" w:rsidR="00362DA4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>The final phase focuses on organizational enablement, transitioning the solution into production, and ensuring sustained adoption.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="697E21F0" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00362DA4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0"/><w:numId w:val="9"/></w:numPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Key Activities:Â Activities are centered on conducting comprehensive end-user training sessions, finalizing go-live preparations, and providing dedicated "</w:t></w:r><w:proofErr w:type="spellStart"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>hypercare</w:t></w:r><w:proofErr w:type="spellEnd"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>" support immediately following the launch to quickly address any operational issues.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="26E8E8B4" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00362DA4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/><w:numPr><w:ilvl w:val="0"/><w:numId w:val="9"/></w:numPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>Milestones &amp; Deliverables:Â The project is officially concluded upon the delivery of finalized Training Materials, the execution of the formal Go-Live Plan, and the acquisition of Final Sign-off from project sponsors, marking the transition to standard operational support.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="2FC703B0" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00362DA4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00362DA4" w:rsidP="00362DA4"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="79D12C5F" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00785050" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00785050" w:rsidP="00664C48"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="3D22D67B" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="00016651" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00016651" w:rsidP="00991A0E"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading1"/><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="32" w:name="_Toc230880929"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:b/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:lastRenderedPageBreak/><w:t>Further information</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="32"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="2341E164" w14:textId="7256FE4F" w:rsidR="00517B54" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00F51833" w:rsidP="00517B54"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="33" w:name="_Toc230880930"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>Future development roadmap</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="33"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="4F2B5E11" w14:textId="77777777" w:rsidR="007A467B" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00F51833" w:rsidP="007A467B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t xml:space="preserve">The future development roadmap for the Account Receivable Intelligence solution is focused on introducing new capabilities that further enhance risk management, operational efficiency, and predictive accuracy. These planned enhancements are designed to expand the solution's value and provide even deeper, more proactive insights for the finance organization. The roadmap includes four key areas for future development: strengthening credit controls, identifying high-risk customers, automating cash </w:t></w:r><w:proofErr w:type="gramStart"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>application</w:t></w:r><w:proofErr w:type="gramEnd"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>, and enriching data for more precise risk profiling.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="647EA749" w14:textId="3568B872" w:rsidR="00FE69E4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00836B0F" w:rsidP="007A467B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/><w:bCs/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="34" w:name="_Toc230880931"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>P</w:t></w:r><w:r w:rsidR="00FE69E4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>rimary enhancements</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="34"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="42BC8845" w14:textId="60238ED5" w:rsidR="007A467B" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="007A467B" w:rsidP="007A467B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>A primary focus of future enhancements is on proactive credit risk mitigation. This will be achieved by developing Credit Limit Breach Indicators, which will provide real-time monitoring of customers exceeding their credit limits. This feature is designed to reduce bad debt risk and offer better visibility to CFOs and risk management teams. In parallel, a High Write-Off Customer Identification model is planned. This classification model will analyze the frequency and value of historical write-offs to automatically flag high-risk accounts early, enabling more targeted and timely intervention before accounts become uncollectible.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="351A73C3" w14:textId="0D78C23C" w:rsidR="00FE69E4" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="00FE69E4" w:rsidP="00FE69E4"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/><w:bCs/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:bookmarkStart w:id="35" w:name="_Toc230880932"/><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:eastAsia="Times New Roman"/><w:bCs/></w:rPr><w:t>Operational efficiency enhancements</w:t></w:r><w:bookmarkEnd w:id="35"/></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="0CC29532" w14:textId="6770075B" w:rsidR="007A467B" w:rsidRPr="009507DD" w:rsidRDefault="007A467B" w:rsidP="00F14E2B"><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="BodyText"/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidRPr="009507DD"><w:t>To improve operational efficiency, an Unapplied Cash Monitoring system is a key planned enhancement. This system will actively monitor unapplied cash and create automated triggers for manual clearing, which will significantly improve the speed and accuracy of the cash application process. Finally, to enrich the solution's predictive capabilities, External Data Integration is a critical part of the future strategy. This involves integrating external data sources, such as credit scores from agencies like Dun &amp; Bradstreet, to create a more comprehensive and accurate customer risk profile. By incorporating this external data, the solution's predictive models will become even more powerful and precise.</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p w14:paraId="5C46605C" w14:textId="5866EF95" w:rsidR="00800F97"
